     629: function RealmTravelCompleteCheckpointBookmarkCheck(lib, cineNumber, strBookmarkName)
     630:   local longName = "BOOKMARK_" .. strBookmarkName
     631:   local varName = "SAVED_" .. longName
     632:   local tableEntry = lib[longName]

     634:     LD.SetEntityVariable(varName, true)

     636:     return true
